- **marker-expression-overlay-visualization**: Use when a spatial single-cell dataset (e.g., CODEX-exported .csv with x,y coordinates) has already been unrolled onto a linear structure and you need to visualize marker expression along organ length and wall thickness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-expression-overlay-visualization))
- **marker-expression-score-ranking**: Use when converting per-cluster differential marker lists (e.g. Seurat FindAllMarkers output with Entrez IDs and scores) into ranked cell-type annotations, where significance-adjusted candidates must be ordered by marker expression significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-expression-score-ranking))
- **marker-feature-identification-and-validation**: Use when when processing GC–MS or LC–MS data as m/z vs retention time chromatograms and you need to identify biomarker or chemical marker features without conventional peak picking, particularly when false positive detection rates from peak detection algorithms are problematic. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/marker-feature-identification-and-validation) · 4 copies)
- **marker-feature-identification-chromatography-ms**: Use when when processing raw chromatography–mass spectrometry data (GC–MS or LC–MS) as a 2D m/z vs retention time map and you need to identify and visualize marker features for analyte discrimination without relying on conventional peak picking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/marker-feature-identification-chromatography-ms) · 3 copies)
- **marker-feature-visualization-retention-time-mz**: Use when after NPFimg's automated detection algorithm has identified marker features from a two-dimensional MS map (m/z vs retention time), especially when you need to validate feature positions, inspect co-localization patterns, or communicate results to stakeholders. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/marker-feature-visualization-retention-time-mz))
- **marker-fidelity-roc-analysis**: Use when you have hybrid cluster assignments from ClonoCluster at multiple α values and need to verify that cluster markers keep equivalent fidelity (median top-marker AUC) versus the α = 0 transcriptome-only baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-fidelity-roc-analysis))
- **marker-file-preparation-panglaodb**: Use when running marker-aware cell selection or active-learning initialization on scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq/CyTOF data and a marker gene set per expected cell type must be sourced, formatted, or validated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-file-preparation-panglaodb))
- **marker-gated-cell-phenotyping**: Use when the input is per-cell x/y coordinates plus multi-marker intensity values (e.g., MCMICRO output from a CyCIF whole-slide image in an AnnData object) and the analysis goal is to label each cell by marker-defined type (e.g., SOX10+ tumor cells) before spatial/region analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gated-cell-phenotyping))
- **marker-gene-alignment-validation**: Use when cells have been reannotated by Annotatability's reannotation module and you must confirm the new labels biologically by checking whether canonical marker expression (e.g., Gad1 for inhibitory neurons) now matches the reassigned cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/marker-gene-alignment-validation))
- **marker-gene-annotation-of-cell-subclusters**: Use when re-clustered scRNA-seq subpopulations must be biologically labeled by signature genes and their purity quantified with a quantitative statistic such as ROGUE before being trusted as subtypes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-annotation-of-cell-subclusters))
- **marker-gene-auc-specificity-comparison**: Use when a single-cell integrated/clustering result has interpretable gene modules (e.g. JOINTLY factors) and you need to test if module genes are more cell-type-specific than conventional marker genes, or to guide cell type annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-auc-specificity-comparison))
- **marker-gene-based-abundance-estimation**: Use when profiling bacteria, archaea, eukaryotes, or viruses from metagenomic shotgun sequencing data, especially when accurate quantification of uncharacterized species (uSGBs/MAGs without cultured isolates) is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/marker-gene-based-abundance-estimation))
- **marker-gene-based-cell-identity-validation**: Use when annotating query scRNA-seq cells against a well-annotated reference and you need to verify predicted identities via reference cell type marker genes, or when ablating the atlas-assisted marker identification step (scMAGIC_no-Atlas) to quantify its contribution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/marker-gene-based-cell-identity-validation))
- **marker-gene-based-cell-type-classification**: Use when scRNA-seq clusters have been identified (e.g., via robustSingleCell PCA/clustering on LCMV1/LCMV2 10X data) and need to be assigned biologically interpretable cell-type labels from a curated marker-gene panel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-based-cell-type-classification--f1456fa7f08ffc0c))
- **marker-gene-based-cell-type-classification**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., T/NK cells from BRCA/KIRC or myeloid cells from PAAD) needs cell-type/subtype annotation via a canonical marker-gene set, or when marker-based tools must be benchmarked against a learned classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-based-cell-type-classification--a8948535cde78edb))
- **marker-gene-based-cell-visualization**: Use when IA-SVA (or similar SV analysis) has identified outlier cells via a surrogate variable, but standard all-gene visualizations (PCA, USVA, tSNE) fail to clearly separate those outlier cells in the embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-based-cell-visualization))
- **marker-gene-based-classification**: Use when benchmarking cell-type label prediction on multi-batch scRNA-seq data, specifically to compare a marker/reference-database classifier such as CellTypist against zero-shot predictors using accuracy and macro-F1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-based-classification))
- **marker-gene-based-cluster-interpretation**: Use when a re-clustering or clustering partition of scRNA-seq data (e.g. low-purity fibroblast clusters split by ROGUE) needs biological labels, and marker genes distinguishing subtypes such as myCAFs or antigen-presenting CAFs can be evaluated from the expression matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-based-cluster-interpretation))
- **marker-gene-based-confident-cell-selection**: Use when building a labeled reference scRNA-seq training set for supervised TME cell subtype annotation and you need to filter cells down to those confidently belonging to T.cells, Myeloid.cells, B.cells, Fibroblast, or Endothelial compartments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-based-confident-cell-selection))
- **marker-gene-based-genome-recovery-assessment**: Use when you have binned contigs (e.g. MetaBAT2 bins from a HiFi metagenome assembly) and need to grade MAG recovery into quality categories from marker-gene completeness/contamination estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/marker-gene-based-genome-recovery-assessment))
- **marker-gene-based-species-quantification**: Use when you need species-level relative-abundance profiles and explainable-read fractions from FASTQ shotgun metagenomes, especially to compare marker versions across environments (human gut, rumen, wild mice, ocean). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/marker-gene-based-species-quantification))
- **marker-gene-branch-assignment**: Use when you have an inferred branching trajectory over ~270 single-cell qPCR hematopoiesis cells (~170 transcription factors, six sorted populations) and need to quantitatively evaluate which branches correspond to marker-expressing lineages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-branch-assignment))
- **marker-gene-cell-fate-validation**: Use when a trajectory-inference run has auto-detected terminal states that must be biologically labeled and validated, especially for rare populations like delta cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-cell-fate-validation))
- **marker-gene-cell-type-annotation**: Use when an scATAC-seq/snap object has been clustered (e.g. Leiden clusters in SnapATAC) and each numeric cluster needs a cell-type label based on marker-gene accessibility or motif activity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-cell-type-annotation))
- **marker-gene-cell-type-annotation**: Use when single-cell count matrices (per-cell gene/isoform counts from scywalker) need cluster-based cell-type labels from marker gene sets, and per-cell-type pseudobulk counts are required per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/marker-gene-cell-type-annotation))
- **marker-gene-cell-type-annotation**: Use when a minor/rare cell cluster has been detected in a large scRNA-seq dataset (e.g. by GapClust on the 68k PBMC matrix) and must be annotated to a cell type via marker-gene expression (CD14, CD34) and benchmarked against GiniClust/FiRE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-cell-type-annotation))
- **marker-gene-collection-and-registration**: Use when SINCERA cell types have been identified for a scRNA-Seq dataset and known positive/negative markers are needed as inputs to signature.prediction for cell type-specific signature prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-collection-and-registration))
- **marker-gene-completeness-contamination-estimation**: Use when binned contig FASTA files (MAGs) have been produced by a binning step and you need per-bin completeness/contamination estimates plus assembly statistics to judge bin quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/marker-gene-completeness-contamination-estimation))
- **marker-gene-curation**: Use when bulk RNA-seq expression data must be decomposed into cell-type composition trends but no snRNA-seq/scRNA-seq reference profile exists and only cell-specific marker genes (gene, cluster, optional fold-change weight) are available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-curation))
- **marker-gene-database-annotation**: Use when annotating clusters or cells in scRNA-seq or spatial (e.g., MERFISH) datasets from human or murine tissues, especially when marker-based annotation consistency and interpretability are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-database-annotation))
- **marker-gene-database-matching**: Use when you have cluster assignments from a scRNA-seq clustering method (e.g. scBGEDA) and need to assign biological cell-type identities by matching cluster DEGs to a curated marker gene database such as CellMark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-database-matching))
- **marker-gene-definition-vectorization**: Use when you need a per-gene statistic that precisely matches the ideal marker-gene definition — a gene high in exactly one subtype and low in all others — across many subtypes in a batch-corrected expression matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-definition-vectorization))
- **marker-gene-detection-cosine-similarity**: Use when a batch-corrected gene/protein expression matrix with per-sample subtype labels is available and you need to identify subtype-specific marker genes across many subtypes, where one-vs-rest statistics (OVR t-test, Limma/EdgeR) yield inaccurate MG. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-detection-cosine-similarity))
- **marker-gene-differential-expression**: Use when a scRNA-seq query has been projected onto a reference atlas (e.g. murine TIL atlas) and you need subtype-level marker genes or perturbation-altered gene programs, such as KO-vs-WT shifts in Tnfrsf9/Ifng/Tcf7/Ccr7. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-differential-expression))
- **marker-gene-enrichment-code-scoring**: Use when an AnnData with log-cpm normalised counts and over-clustered Leiden labels needs per-cluster marker genes and cell-by-cluster enrichment scores, e.g. to judge which cluster pairs are significantly different before merging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-enrichment-code-scoring))
- **marker-gene-expression-overlay-plotting**: Use when you have a learned latent embedding (e.g. scMDC Z from CITE-seq data like BMNC or multi-batch SLN111) and need to visually verify cell-type separation, marker-gene concordance, and batch-effect removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-expression-overlay-plotting))
- **marker-gene-expression-projection**: Use when a PAGA-clustered single-cell reprogramming dataset needs visual validation that annotated clusters show expected marker enrichment, e.g., Apoa1 in iEP clusters and Col1a2 in fibroblast clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-expression-projection))
- **marker-gene-expression-validation**: Use when clusters have been assigned by SINCERA cluster.assignment and you must confirm predicted cell types are consistent with selectively expressed known marker genes before exporting assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-expression-validation--2b8c476836ecc6ea))
- **marker-gene-expression-validation**: Use when BUSseq has been fit to scRNA-seq count data (e.g., the mouse hematopoietic study) and unknown clusters must be assigned biological identity via lineage marker genes and reference cell-type atlas correlations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-expression-validation--ed22bf86b565e760))
- **marker-gene-expression-visualization**: Use when scRNA-Seq clusters or predicted cell type-specific signatures need quality assessment via known marker gene expression patterns, e.g. after cluster.assignment or signature.prediction in SINCERA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-expression-visualization))
- **marker-gene-expression-visualization-across-clusters**: Use when scRNA-seq count matrices (raw vs imputed) must be compared at the cluster level by coloring t-SNE plots by cell type and overlaying curated marker genes (e.g., S100A8, FCER1A) to confirm subpopulation separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-expression-visualization-across-clusters))
- **marker-gene-identification**: Use when you need marker gene sets for Cell Ontology cell types — including types with no annotated cells in training data — from OnClass prediction matrices and single-cell expression data such as Tabula Muris Senis FACS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-identification))
- **marker-gene-identification-across-clusters**: Use when you have a clustered scRNA-seq matrix and need cluster-level marker genes free of pseudo markers (upregulated in multiple clusters) before automatic cell type annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-identification-across-clusters))
- **marker-gene-identification-by-clustering**: Use when you have a processed single-cell expression matrix (AnnData object) with pre-computed cluster assignments (e.g., leiden or louvain clusters in adata.obs) and want to discover which genes define each cluster's transcriptional identity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/marker-gene-identification-by-clustering))
- **marker-gene-identification-for-immune-cell-populations**: Use when an unsupervised clustering of scRNA-seq data (e.g., k-means on PCs of PBMCs) yields clusters whose biological identities must be determined from cluster-enriched genes such as CD3D, NKG7, CD79A, S100A8/S100A9, PF4, FCER1A. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-identification-for-immune-cell-populations))
- **marker-gene-identification-reference-scrnaseq**: Use when building a reference-based single-cell classifier that needs cell type-specific marker genes validated not only within the reference dataset but also against an independent atlas (e.g. MCA), so markers are specific across a broader cell-type space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/marker-gene-identification-reference-scrnaseq))
- **marker-gene-identification-via-rsq-regression**: Use when an IA-SVA run has produced a significant surrogate variable (e.g., SV1 for 101 human alpha cells) and you need the marker genes driving that hidden factor for downstream interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-identification-via-rsq-regression))
- **marker-gene-list-validation**: Use when a dimensionality-reduction/feature-selection method produces a ranked gene list that must be checked for biological plausibility against known cell-type markers before downstream use. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-list-validation))
- **marker-gene-metabarcoding-processing**: Use when starting from raw paired-end NextSeq FASTQ files of 16S or COI amplicons and the goal is an error-filtered, merged, chimera-removed MOTU count table per marker gene. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/marker-gene-metabarcoding-processing))
- **marker-gene-overlap-analysis**: Use when a clustering method outputs per-cluster key genes and you must verify they are biologically meaningful by testing overlap with published cell-type markers and DEGs (|logFC| &gt; 2, adjusted p &lt; 0.01). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-overlap-analysis))
- **marker-gene-pair-cox-survival-regression**: Use when you have bulk transcriptomic profiles (RNA-seq or microarray) with matched survival information and a cell-type marker set, and need to identify prognostic marker gene pairs as the basis for inferring cell-cell interactions in the tumor immune microenvironment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-pair-cox-survival-regression))
- **marker-gene-pair-score-matrix-construction**: Use when you have a bulk transcriptomic profile (RNA-seq or microarray) plus a cell-type marker annotation and need expression-level pairwise relations as input to prognostic gene-pair analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-pair-score-matrix-construction))
- **marker-gene-pairwise-comparison**: Use when you have a cell-type marker gene list (e.g., scRNA-seq-derived T cell subtype markers) plus bulk RNA-seq/microarray data and need expression-level pairwise relations as input to survival modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-pairwise-comparison))
- **marker-gene-percent-expressing-aggregation**: Use when validating scRNA-seq imputation on marker genes: you have a cell-by-gene normalized matrix (and optionally its imputed counterpart), cell-type labels, and need per-type percent-expressing fractions for genes like NCAM1 or CR2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-percent-expressing-aggregation))
- **marker-gene-percentile-selection**: Use when preparing positive/negative marker files for BIDCell training on spatial transcriptomics data (e.g., Xenium breast cancer), or when defining cell-type markers by expression percentile cutoffs from a single-cell reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-percentile-selection))
- **marker-gene-ranking-by-log2fc**: Use when Louvain/SNN cluster labels exist per cell and you must characterize each cluster by its top differentially expressed genes, e.g. to reproduce per-cluster marker depictions like the paper's Fig. 2E. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-ranking-by-log2fc))
- **marker-gene-recovery-validation**: Use when a redPATH pseudotime ordering exists for single-cell RNA-seq data and the goal is to discover and validate genes whose expression significantly associates with both cell proliferation and differentiation along the trajectory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-recovery-validation))
- **marker-gene-score-computation-from-rna-velocity-layers**: Use when an AnnData annotated with DeepCycle's obs['cell_cycle_theta'] needs marker-gene-derived phase-transition landmarks (Cyclin E peak, G2M-vs-S crossover) to assign cells to G1, S, or G2/M. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-score-computation-from-rna-velocity-layers))
- **marker-gene-selection-per-cell-type**: Use when you have a pre-annotated reference scRNA-seq dataset and need to learn cell-type marker lists (with rejection thresholds) for downstream reference-based cell type identification instead of using a curated marker DB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-selection-per-cell-type))
- **marker-gene-sensitivity-scoring**: Use when NeuCA's hierarchical branch is triggered — off-diagonal Pearson correlations among cell-type mean profiles exceed tau = 0.95 — and test cells must be pre-assigned from highly sensitive markers before tree traversal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-sensitivity-scoring))
- **marker-gene-set-preparation**: Use when you need cell-type marker gene sets (positive and negative) from the ScTypeDB database restricted to a specific tissue type (e.g. 'Immune system', 'Liver', 'Eye', 'Brain') before running sctype_score or comparable marker-based annotation methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-set-preparation))
- **marker-gene-specificity-scoring**: Use when harmonizing cell-type marker genes from multiple annotation sources (e.g., CellMarker2.0, PanglaoDB) and you need consistent, evidence-weighted marker scores for downstream cell type annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/marker-gene-specificity-scoring))
- **marker-gene-taxonomic-profiling**: Use when raw metagenomic sequencing reads (FASTQ) from microbiome samples must be converted into per-sample taxonomic abundance profiles and functional gene-family/KO tables for downstream statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/marker-gene-taxonomic-profiling))
